|------------|--------------------|-------------|------|-------|

Counting rule: entities at r ≥ 35 from plaza center, quadrant by sign of
(x, z); count entities whose id or comps carry lamp/light/glow/flame
anchors. Budget = live count (never exceed existing density).

### dress-103 — NE great bellows (round-3 item 9, BUILD-ONLY — v6 judged 3/5, v7 + placement owed next tick)

- **Concept contract**: the forge's AIR MACHINE — the third element of the
  smithy beside the anvil pad (strike) and the billet (heat). A
  workshop-scale double-board bellows on a timber trestle cradle, nozzle
  aimed at the forge firebox, depicted MID-STROKE: SOLID leather chamber
  swelling tall toward the nose, thick top board riding it, steel
  gathering band at the nose, pump bar framed between two GROUNDED posts
  (the smith's lever). Host: CORE forge `nx-forge` (22.118, 0, −7.958)
  yaw −0.9076, firebox mouth + coals on its local +z face (0, 0.45,
  0.46). The local −x flank is court-annexed (R-105); local +x is the
  anvil alley (dress-76, 1.4m law); so the seat band is forge-local
  **(+0.6..+1.6, +1.6..+2.4)** — beside the forge wall off the working
  face's shoulder, nozzle −x toward the coals. CORE siting by commission
  (item 9 anchors at the core forge — rim law does not apply; r≈24-27
  recorded). Static, no comps; ONE mesh-ember bead at the nozzle drop
  (emissive MATERIAL, not a light entity) — NE lamp budget untouched
  (0 of 10).
- **Build** (`assets/mkv3-dress-ne-bellows1.ts`): six-version arc, every
  version deterministically rebuilt ×2 and decode-audited first
  (`dress103-decode-bellows.ts` — bucket-by-COLOR_0 decoder; GLTFLoader
  maps COLOR_0 to `geometry.attributes.color` and glbwrite bakes LINEAR
  bytes, so keys derive via THREE.Color, lessons banked in the decode
  header):
  - v1 `152e83b9` decode-CAUGHT shallow plan-wedge (taper 0.706 > 0.7).
  - v2 `9c8afac5` decode ALL PASS; native 18m FAIL ×2 — thin rod lever
    read as a CANNON BARREL; mid-grey iron merged into the wood. A
    factual pixel probe of the frames CONFIRMED the judge before any
    redesign (both directions, probe-discipline law).
  - v3 `8efd31fa` steel promotions + lever shortened — STILL floating
    past the body ([−0.54, +0.08] vs body edge −0.55). FAIL, correctly.
  - v4/v5 `0dd00530` reversed anatomy (hinge thin at butt, chamber tall
    at nose, raised board) — decode ALL PASS incl. new ELEVATION-wedge
    law; native FAIL: lever-center math error (bar span [−0.47, +0.17],
    0.72m past the body) + open gap under a 0.06 board reads as slab.
  - v6 `458cf886` **ACCEPTED-PENDING-VALUE** — SOLID leather chamber
    (mass over gap, dress-97 silhouette law), thick 0.10 top board,
    lever = pump bar spanning hinge-post to a SECOND GROUNDED POST
    (dress-14 ne-yard precedent: a horizontal member past a mass ends on
    a grounded vertical), steel band widened 0.14 × full nose height.
    Decode ALL PASS 12/12 (grounded 0.000, h 0.82, footprint 1.96m²,
    10 nodes, plan+taper wedges, gusset inset, nozzle droop, ember at
    nozzle, lever-low-pole 0.75, steel at nose −1.91). Native 18m:
    **IDENTITY PASS** (wedge-on-stand bellows silhouette), **MACHINERY
    PASS** (bar between two grounded posts reads lever frame), 
    GROUNDING PASS; **VALUE FAIL** (steel band still not separating —
    the nozzle end sits dark) + **NOISE FAIL** (the three-lump coal
    scatter reads as a hairline debris streak at 18m).
  - **v7 (this tick, dress-104)**: applied the two owed fixes (STEEL tip
    promotion; band 0.24 long + y 0.38..1.04 proud) — but the v7 decode
    run FAILED on its own new laws, and the vertex probe proved the
    `frustum()` helper broken since **v1**: it never scaled x (every

    (cancelling face-height variation — v6's "thick top board" was a
    tilted ZERO-THICKNESS sheet; the leather's nose face sat 0.09m low;
    the band floated 0.02–0.07m ahead of the true board nose in every

Laws: each installation gets its own authored build (no kit stamping),
18m identity read + contact-path walk where relevant, per-piece eye-check
pointer reported to Bill after placement. If a family proves weak on
placement, DROP it with reason rather than shipping filler — scope is
expanded, the quality bar is not lowered.

## Siting log

### dress-105 — SE dry-stream stone run (round-3 item 13, PLACED, LIVE)

- **Concept contract**: the SE wild's hydrology made visible — a seasonal
  drainage line remembered as a meandering ribbon of water-worn stones on
  a dark sediment bed, mossy at the forest head, dissolving into field at
  the tail. Distinct from se-stones (piles), cairns (waymarks),
  mossbowls (basins): identity = ONE CONTINUOUS WANDERING RUN. Static,
  unlit — SE spend 0 of 8.
- **Build arc** (`assets/mkv3-dress-se-streamstones1.ts`): v1 `768633e6`
  single-file line, native 18m straight-edging FAIL; v2 `629391e9`
  2-row + width rhythm, still straight (perpendicular vantage collapsed
  the meander); v3 `bd2f29ec` value raise — judge VALUE claims
  DISPROVEN by pixel probe (lum bands 0–127, light band 410 px);
  v4 `80867cc7` sediment bed ribbon added — three judges claimed
  bed-absent, probe found 1998 bed px (disclosed; the bed renders, the
  grazing 5° vantage hides it); v5 `aabbc23d` bend-following deposition
  (secondaries sit INSIDE bends, drop at crossings — the v2–v4 strict
  alternation read "angular zigzag," a real judge-caught defect) +
  amplitude 1.5. Deterministic ×2; decode ALL PASS (5 nodes, envelope
  x −4.155..4.531 / z ±2.127, h 0.287 below-knee, 15/15 continuity
  slices, anchor density 721/764 vs median 346).
- **Vantage law banked (dress-105)**: a plan-view S-meander CANNOT read
  from a 5°-down grazing vantage at 18m — the oblique judge fails
  (MEANDER/VALUE) on the same bytes an endon/plan judge passes (bed
  present, stone mix present, bend offsets visible). Judge-by-vantage:
  endon + aerial + live-seat walker render carry the verdict; pixel
  probes settle judge-vs-source disputes in both directions.
- **Siting** (`dress105-streamstones-scan.ts`, census 285): only 4 legal
  poses in the whole SE ring (244 SAT deaths — the ring is saturated).
  Concept-first pick **(75.05, −46.90) yaw −45°, az −32 r 88.5,
  spokeOff 19.9m**: minGap 2.28 to nx-wild-forest-0057 itself (the moss
  head's host, local +x end 6.7m from its trunk mass), wayside-0056
  +2.37, cairn-0043 +3.31, no sub-1.4m adjacency. Rim corners
  85.6..91.7 ∈ [70,100] (CITY-PLAN district band). h 0.287 < knee —
  cone-exempt (231 cone-clash poses disclosed in scan, none binding).
  Terrain 7pt spread 8.8mm → PY −0.0249 (worst corner).
- **Placement** (`se-dress105-place.ts`, dress-94/102 chassis): hash
  gate → blocker-epoch guard [forest-0057, wayside-0056, cairn-0047,
  cairn-0043] → fresh SAT (clear) + rim (in-band) → upload (no 429) →
  1 spawn verb → PLACED_VERIFIED @ store/aabbc23d5552b01c.glb →
  idempotent 0-verb rerun. Post census 286, comps empty.
- **Live-seat verification** (`dress105-live-seat-render.ts`, exact
  seated tuple + forest host): walker 18m — 4/4 PASS (grounded at the
  forest edge, wandering ribbon reads, stream-bed scale, zero defects);
  aerial + 6m frames banked. Judge NATIVE throughout this tick (zero
  1210 flaps).
- **Eye-check for Bill**: SE spoke, ~89m out — where the walker's line
  meets the eastern tree clump, a ribbon of stones on a dark bed flows
  OUT of the forest and wanders into the open meadow, mossy where it
